2026-10-28T07:27:23.8339728Z         """Recursively copy a directory tree and return the destination directory.
2026-10-28T07:27:23.8340108Z     
2026-10-28T07:27:23.8340383Z         If exception(s) occur, an Error is raised with a list of reasons.
2026-10-28T07:27:23.8340730Z     
2026-10-28T07:27:23.8340982Z         If the optional symlinks flag is true, symbolic links in the
2026-10-28T07:27:23.8341404Z         source tree result in symbolic links in the destination tree; if
2026-10-28T07:27:23.8341831Z         it is false, the contents of the files pointed to by symbolic
2026-10-28T07:27:23.8342248Z         links are copied. If the file pointed by the symlink doesn't
2026-10-28T07:27:23.8342670Z         exist, an exception will be added in the list of errors raised in
2026-10-28T07:27:23.8343079Z         an Error exception at the end of the copy process.
2026-10-28T07:27:23.8343440Z     
2026-10-28T07:27:23.8343952Z         You can set the optional ignore_dangling_symlinks flag to true if you
2026-10-28T07:27:23.8344734Z         want to silence this exception. Notice that this has no effect on
2026-10-28T07:27:23.8345310Z         platforms that don't support os.symlink.
2026-10-28T07:27:23.8345588Z     
2026-10-28T07:27:23.8345833Z         The optional ignore argument is a callable. If given, it
2026-10-28T07:27:23.8346243Z         is called with the `src` parameter, which is the directory
2026-10-28T07:27:23.8346651Z         being visited by copytree(), and `names` which is the list of

2026-10-28T07:27:23.8347814Z

2026-10-28T07:27:23.8348625Z         called once for each directory that is copied. It returns a
2026-10-28T07:27:23.8349030Z         list of names relative to the `src` directory that should

2026-10-28T07:27:23.8349568Z

2026-10-28T07:27:23.8350302Z         to copy each file. It will be called with the source path and the
2026-10-28T07:27:23.8350905Z         destination path as arguments. By default, copy2() is used, but any
2026-10-28T07:27:23.8351638Z         function that supports the same signature (like copy()) can be used.
2026-10-28T07:27:23.8352250Z

2026-10-28T07:27:23.8353885Z         operation will continue if it encounters existing directories, and files
2026-10-28T07:27:23.8354384Z         within the `dst` tree will be overwritten by corresponding files from the
2026-10-28T07:27:23.8354758Z         `src` tree.
